The resulting balance sheet is written into CH20: matter cashed for a rush-buy returns ~0.2 Production per unit — a five-to-one loss against the Hall's own output — so Bullion is liquidity, not an economy, and the physics' "at a loss" now emerges from Unciv's arithmetic rather than being asserted.

### v3.35 — Re-locking Priced Against Gold; a Truncation Bug Caught

- At v3.34's rates a permanent +2 strategic resource cost the equivalent of 30 Gold and a permanent luxury 40 — absurd against Civ V, where the AI wants ~240 Gold for one luxury for thirty turns. All re-locking is now priced against what Bullion would return: strategic resources 240 Prima Materia (≈180 Gold), luxuries 400 (≈300 Gold), Bullion 40 → 30 Gold (three-quarters, still a visible loss; the Stone round-trip yields three-eighths, so no pump). The anchor is written into the row: gold must never be the weak use of matter. Turnings rise to 60 Qi and the Heat Exchange to 40 Caloric on the same reasoning.
- **Bug caught by the same check:** the converter truncates to whole units (`amount.toInt()`), and the canonical Forge has no flat production, so Formless Reserve's "25% of every Forge's output" would have yielded zero for the whole game. It now converts the Workshop's whole flat output (2 a turn). Rule added to §7.0: never convert a percentage of a small or absent flat stat.

### v3.34 — The Converter Duplicates: Losses Moved to Where They Really Are

- **Checked in code, as asked:** `[X]% of [stat] from every [buildingFilter] in the city added to [resource]` is applied in `CityResources.kt`, where it reads a building's stat and adds a resource supply; nothing in `CityStats.kt` subtracts the stat. It duplicates rather than diverts. The Transmutation Hall therefore yields formless matter *alongside* its production, and the Philosopher's Stone draws matter from Market gold for free. Both rows now say so, and the physics' loss is placed where the mechanics can honour it — in re-locking: Transmuted resources cost 60 Prima Materia (was 40), luxuries 80, and Bullion returns **20 Gold for 40 matter** (two for one), so gold → matter → gold yields a quarter and the Stone cannot pump. The "banked production" claim is withdrawn; what a Substance civilisation has is a *second treasury* of matter.
- For the Process world the duplication is the design: the Cycle Forge keeps its production *and* yields Qi — the opened loop's gain, literally. Turning costs raised to 40 Qi to compensate.
- `Building.getStats` feeds the converter only a building's own `[stats]` uniques, and no city-terrain conditional exists, so the Caloric desert bonus becomes a separate Desert-sited **Solar Reservoir** (+6, `Must be on [Desert]`) rather than a conditional. §7.0 corrected to describe the converter accurately.

### v3.33 — Bullion Buys Friends

- Read against the code rather than memory, unit gifting is not uncertain: `UnitActions.getGiftActions` lets a *civilian* be gifted to a City-State when it carries `Gain [amount] Influence with a [baseUnitFilter] gift to a City-State`, paying that Influence on delivery inside the City-State's borders. Transmuted Bullion now carries `Gain [15] Influence with a [Transmuted Bullion] gift to a City-State` — so the wagon has a second use and a real reason to travel, and the capture risk that v3.32 removed returns with a mechanic behind it. Instant gold, or a slower and richer road to alliance; Influence cannot be converted back, so the exchange stays pump-safe.

### v3.32 — Bullion Is a Conversion, Not a Caravan

- Gold is a civilisation-wide treasury, so Transmuted Bullion is built and consumed in the same city on the same turn; the "capturable wagon" flavour described nothing that could happen. The row now says what the unit is — a repeatable conversion in unit form, consumed on the spot. A City-State route was considered and rejected: the trade-mission template fixes its gold by era and would break the pump-safe exchange rate.

### v3.31 — Bullion and Banked Production; Heat Follows the Terrain

- **Transmuted Bullion** (CH20): a consumable Civilian unit bought with 40 Prima Materia that cashes as 40 Gold anywhere — the Great Merchant's `<by consuming this unit>` mechanism, with the `[stockpile]` parameter's native acceptance of stat names. This gives Substance civilisations something no Civ V empire has: **banked production**. Surplus becomes matter in peace and is cashed to buy units instantly in war, at a loss (purchase prices run 2–4 Gold per Production), and the bullion wagon is capturable on the road. With the Philosopher's Stone the circle closes — gold to matter to iron, or to gold again — and the rates are set pump-safe: the Stone returns half of Market gold as matter, Bullion returns matter as gold one-for-one, so a round trip loses half. Tune Bullion *below* one-to-one, never above.
- **Caloric follows the terrain.** The Reservoir banks 6 Caloric a turn in a Desert city instead of 4 (`[+2 Production] in cities on [Desert] tiles`, converted); the Heat Exchange gives +2 Food and +1 Happiness extra on Snow and +1/+1 on Tundra — heat is worth most where there is least of it. The Reservoir's own cold-city happiness moved to the Exchange, where it belongs. One verification point recorded for the fork: the stat-to-stockpile converter must count conditional building stats.

### v3.30 — Phlogiston and Caloric Worlds Made Whole

- **Phlogiston worlds** lost Oxygen Theory's furnace bonus at the dead-end with nothing to replace it, and had only the Airship to show for the truth. Phlogiston Extraction now carries super-combustion (`[+2 Production] from all [Forge] buildings`, matching and re-labelling the oxygen-world bonus), and Levity Gas adds the **Levity Transport** — an airship that carries a land unit, so a phlogiston army crosses mountains and seas in the age of muskets. The branch's identity is levity and heat, and both are now visible in play; no resource needed.
- **Caloric worlds** had lost their identity outright: "heat as a storable, tradeable substance" was the point of the branch, and the v3.29 cut left a siege unit and a happiness building. The mistake was building it as a map-style strategic; it is a **stockpile**. The Caloric Reservoir banks its whole output as Caloric; the **Heat Exchange** spends it (+3 Production, +2 Happiness, only in cities *without* a Reservoir — a city banks or draws); the Caloric Lance and Thermal Ward cost Caloric; the Caloric Engine Plant is bought with it and runs fuel-free. Four sinks, no new interface, and Caloric Theory now carries the Steam Works bonus that Laws of Thermodynamics dead-ends. §7.0 updated.

### v3.29 — Strategic Resource Audit

**Every resource this design added was tested against one standard: at least three consumers spanning an era, and a game honestly poorer without it.** Outcome (recorded in §7.0):
- **Kept:** Saltpeter (Musketman, Cannon, Fireworks Ground — a Renaissance scarcity Civ V lacked, with Riflemen as the free fallback exactly as Pikemen are for iron); Rare Earths (six consumers, v3.28); Obsidian (the volcanic cluster's payoff); the Prima Materia and Qi stockpiles; the Heavy Ore alias.
- **Cut as resources:** Sulfur and Bitumen — two more gates on eras already gated, and double jeopardy for the gunpowder age. Sulfur Mining survives as knowledge (bleaching, antiseptic, the Gunpowder prerequisite); Bitumen becomes **Pitch and Tar** (wood tar from the charcoal kiln — road maintenance and caulked hulls), which needs no deposit and no world flag. FOSSIL_FUELS_ABSENT now removes only Coal and Oil; Greek Fire (pine pitch, sulfur, a siphon) exists in every world.
- **Cut:** Rubber (nothing consumed it) — Rubber Discovery (MI09) removed, Vulcanization now requires Botanical Classification; Phlogiston and Caloric as resources (one consumer each) — the Levity Airship and Caloric Lance now require their producing building in the city; Ritual Bronze (the Foundry Temple provides Copper). ~532 → ~531.

### v3.28 — Rare Earths Made a Real Strategic Resource

- Rare Earths (added v3.22) was a strategic resource nothing consumed — a prerequisite for Miniaturisation and a tile science yield, no more. It is now the late-game high-tech strategic, `requiredResource` for the Microchip Foundry, Fab, Photonic Network, Quantum Computer and Drone (and the Solar Farm when Energy Systems is grounded), in the role Aluminum plays for aircraft. This also gives Transmuted Rare Earths (CH20) something worth buying, and makes the resource a genuine diplomatic and military object.

### v3.27 — No Conjured Animals; the Cycle Forge as the Mirror of Transmutation

- **Transmuted Horses removed** (a vat should not produce a living thing); **Transmuted Rare Earths** takes the slot — minerals only. Transmuted Coal and Oil are removed with their resources in FOSSIL_FUELS_ABSENT worlds.
- **The Cycle Forge re-designed as the exact mirror of the Transmutation Hall.** Its Forest/Jungle yields were both dull and a start-position lottery. Now the Forge converts its own production into **Qi**, a second stockpiled resource — at 150%, the opened loop's gain — and Qi buys **the Five Turnings** (Wood +Food, Fire +Production, Earth +Gold, Metal +Science, Water +Culture), each requiring the one before, so the wheel turns only forward. Substance: a stockpile lost, spent on any form. Process: a stockpile gained, spent along a fixed sequence. Neither depends on terrain. A city that completes all five Turnings has "turned the wheel", which Great Harmony (QM-E11) will key off when its focus is grounded.
- Tuning note: 30 Qi is five turns of one Forge for a +3-yield building — deliberately generous, since the gain *is* the Process world's identity; raise toward 40 if it outpaces normal construction in play.

### v3.26 — Transmutation as a Stockpile; Decisive Experiments as Laboratories

- **Transmutation re-based on Unciv's native stockpiled resources.** "Provides Iron and Horses in every city" was creation, not re-tempering, and a glut. Now: the Transmutation Hall disperses its own production into **Prima Materia** (a `Stockpiled` resource — formless temperament), and seven small "Transmuted" buildings re-lock it into the form you lack (`Costs [40] [Prima Materia]` → `Provides [2] [Iron]`, likewise Horses, Coal, Oil, Aluminum; `Costs [60]` → Jewelry or Porcelain, the luxuries that are made rather than mined). Any form into any other, at a loss, with no new interface — the stockpile is in the resource bar, the costs in the build menu. The Philosopher's Stone grants a lump of Prima Materia and turns Market gold into matter; Formless Reserve catches a quarter of every Forge's output as Prima Materia. The Cycle Forge no longer conjures ore: its gain is production from Forest and Jungle plus a city-wide bonus — forward-only and renewable, as the Process physics says.
- **The five decisive experiments are now national-wonder laboratories.** Each is one-per-civilisation, costs production, and on completion `Triggers a [event] event` (native) carrying the world's report, then `Reveals` (fork unique) the breakthrough this world's answer opens — Periodic Table / Transmutation / Five-Phase for the Matter axis, Germ / Miasma / Humoral for Disease, and so on. The breakthrough is the gate; building the laboratory is the choice to find out. Native event trigger, custom reveal — both recorded in §7.0.
- **§7.0 now enumerates the fork's custom uniques** (six: focus unlock, Insight %, Reveals, obsolescence, Experiment/Synthesis, diffusion phases) so that everything else in the Unlocks column is guaranteed to be a real template; stockpiled resources added to the "what exists" list.

### v3.25 — Mechanical Grounding, Batch 5 (Chemistry, Optics, Electricity, Electronics, Computing)

- **74 breakthroughs rewritten concretely.** Chemistry's canonical mine bonus lands on Acid/Base Discovery; the canonical Fertilizer farm bonus moves to Catalysis (the Haber process) and Atmospheric Science gets a Weather Bureau instead; Portland Cement carries Machinery's `Improves movement speed on roads`; the Power Grid uses `[in all cities connected to capital]` for both production and happiness — the grid follows the roads.
- **Transmutation and the Generative Cycle made concrete.** Unciv has no resource stockpiles, so "convert resources" becomes the Recycling Center pattern: the Transmutation Hall and Cycle Forge each `Provide` strategic resources — the Hall on demand at a loss (any-to-any, Substance), the Forge from renewable Forest input at a gain (forward-only, Process). The Philosopher's Stone is a wonder that provides Iron and Aluminum outright.
- **The decisive experiments are laboratories** (Combustion, Analytical, Synthesis, Calorimetry), each granting science and revealing the path their result opens — the epistemic ladder as buildings.
- Sentient AI becomes an `Events.json` chain (Unciv cannot spawn a civilisation); Piezoelectricity grants a Sonar promotion; Glassmaking's "Sand" prerequisite becomes a Desert or Coast tile.
- Science percentages from techs are now explicit and should be watched in tuning: Scientific Method +25%, Printing Press +10%, Internet +10%, AGI +15% — Insight is science, so these compound the whole tree's pace.

### v3.24 — Nation-Unique Layering; Mechanical Grounding, Batch 4 (Trade, Textiles, Water Management, Mechanics, Thermodynamics)

- **Layering principle added to §7.0**: players still choose an Unciv nation with its own UA/UU/UB, so UNIQUE breakthroughs are a second layer of identity. A breakthrough never grants a nation's own unique object; nation uniques keep their base object's gate; stacking a nation ability with a matching UNIQUE is allowed and bounded by the cumulative UNIQUE penalty. Eleven rows corrected accordingly: Mangudai (not Keshik), Huscarl (not Berserker), Repeating Crossbowman (not Chu-Ko-Nu), Siphon Galley (not Dromon), no Samurai or Tercio or Battering Ram granted, Chinampa Garden (not Floating Gardens), Terrace Granary (not Terrace farm), Elephant Cavalry / Armoured Elephant (India's War Elephant keeps its name).
- **70 breakthroughs rewritten concretely.** Highlights: `Enables Research agreements` on Free Trade Agreements; Refrigeration's `[15]% Food is carried over after population increases [in all cities]` (the spoilage conversion, empire-wide); Silk Production *provides* the Silk luxury from a building; Mainspring Energy Storage's `<in cities without a [Water Mill]>` (spring power exactly where water gives none); the Automaton as a Civilian unit with `[-100]% maintenance costs`; Caloric worlds gain **Caloric** as a tradeable strategic resource. The Wheel now carries the civilian-movement bonus (removed from Horse Domestication, which gets Horses-tile production instead).
- Validator extended to check **conditionals** as well as uniques (173 conditional templates); all 51 in use are legal.

### v3.23 — Mechanical Grounding, Batch 3 (Seafaring, Warfare, Mathematics, Philosophy, Medicine); Arsenical Bronze Removed

- **Arsenical Bronze (ME03) removed**: with tin gone from the resource model its purpose ("bronze without tin trade") no longer exists. **Statistics corrected:** the five "-W" fossil-fuel alternatives had never been counted by the tally script, so every total since v3.2 was five short — the true count is ~532 breakthroughs (72 UNCOMMON).
- **85 breakthroughs rewritten concretely.** Canonical uniques placed where history puts them: `Enables embarkation for land units` on Dugout Canoe, `Enables [Embarked] units to enter ocean tiles` on Celestial Navigation, the Great Wall's `Enemy [Land] units must spend [1] extra movement points…` as the Fortified Border culture, `Great General provides double combat bonus` as the Mongol culture, the Honor-style `Earn [50]% of killed [Military] unit's [Strength] as [Culture]` as Bushido.
- **Scientific Method's bonus is now a legal unique**: Insight *is* science, so `[+25]% [Science] [in all cities]` says exactly what the design meant.
- New units where none existed: Phalanx, Longship, Dhow, Reed Boat, Ekranoplan, Air Rifleman, Railgun Battery, Drone; Musketman now requires Saltpeter, Cannon Sulfur. *(This pass originally granted nation-unique units — Keshik, Samurai, Berserker, Chu-Ko-Nu, Tercio, Dromon — to matching breakthroughs; reversed in v3.24, see below.)*
- **Unit lines spread by era in §7** so no Ancient breakthrough unlocks a Medieval unit: Knight and Pikeman to Steel, Lancer to Pike and Shot, Cavalry stays at Rifling, Great War Infantry to Precision Machining, Infantry to Polymer Science, Machine Gun to Assembly Line, Marine to the Helicopter, Anti-Tank Gun to Internal Combustion. Hospital (+5 Food) moves from Ancient Surgery to Germ Theory; Research Lab from Early-Modern Empiricism to the Periodic Table.
- Temperament Engineering becomes four mutually exclusive Regimen Halls (the Solar/Nuclear Plant exclusivity pattern) — the design's "choose your city's humour", made concrete.

### v3.22 — Mechanical Grounding, Batch 2 (Farming, Animal Husbandry, Mining, Metallurgy, Construction)

**80 breakthroughs rewritten concretely**, same method and validator as v3.21.
- Resources now carry the reveal moments the tree always promised: Horses → AH03, Coal → MI06, Oil → MI07, Uranium → MI10, Aluminum → ME13 (each the canonical `revealedBy`, moved to our breakthrough); Silver and Salt → Deep Shaft Mining (the "second reveal"); and five **new strategic resources** the world-truth design needs — Sulfur (MI04), Bitumen (MI05B), Saltpeter (MI08), Rubber (MI09), Rare Earths (MI11) — plus the Ritual Bronze luxury. FOSSIL_FUELS_ABSENT now means Coal, Oil and Bitumen are simply never placed.
- Canonical uniques land where they belong: `Roads connect tiles across rivers` on Arch Construction; the Fertilizer-class farm and pasture bonuses split across Irrigation (fresh-water farms), Crop Rotation (Wheat), Selective Breeding (Plantations) and Nitrogen Fixation (dry farms); `Provides [2] [Iron]`/`[Aluminum]` on the Asteroid Mining Station (the Recycling Center pattern).
- Civ V has no tin: Bronze Alloy now requires Copper in territory under the Section 1.2 resource rule, and Arsenical Bronze becomes the copper-only defensive alternative. Civ V has no seismic zones: Earthquake-Resistant Design and Earthquake Mastery are re-based on Hill and Mountain country.
- UNIQUE breakthroughs follow the UB + UA pattern throughout (e.g. Flood Harvest → Nilometer + Flood-plains yields; Steppe Rider → Horse Archer + Nomad Camp + `[+1] Movement <for [Mounted] units>`; Ceremonial Road → Pilgrimage Route + `[+1 Culture, +1 Faith] [in all cities connected to capital]`).
- New objects this batch: 27 buildings (incl. 9 unique replacements, 5 national wonders), 6 improvements, 7 units, 3 promotions, 6 resources.

### v3.21 — Mechanical Grounding, Batch 1 (Fire, Foraging, Stonework, Observation, Social Organisation)

**The Unlocks column is being rewritten against Unciv's actual capabilities, read from the repository (`docs/Modders/uniques.md`, `Unique-parameters.md`, the Mod-file-structure docs, and the Gods & Kings ruleset JSONs).**
- **New §7.0 Mechanical Grounding**: the legal vocabulary (seven stats, object fields, Global/Triggerable tech uniques), a conversion table for every concept earlier drafts used that Unciv lacks (tourism, loyalty, morale, health, disasters, weather, city size caps, spoilage, pollution, MAD, probe movement, blockade, code-breaking…), the terrain the tree assumes but Civ V lacks (a new **Volcanic** feature; Flood plains reused; earthquakes dropped), a **calibration rule** (a breakthrough ≈ one-sixth of a canonical tech, CORE gateways excepted; UNIQUEs mirror a Civ V civilisation's UB + UA), and a notation legend.
- **67 breakthroughs rewritten concretely** across the five starting foci — every effect now a base-game object, a new object with a JSON-ready spec, a legal unique in Unciv syntax, a resource `revealedBy`, or a focus unlock. Examples: Controlled Fire → `[+1 Production] [in all cities]` + Tundra food; Ore Identification → sets `revealedBy` for Copper, Gold Ore, Silver, Gems and Iron (hidden until then) + Mine + Mining; Geological Observation → the Triggerable `Reveal up to [all] [Strategic resource] within a [10] tile radius`; Guilds → the canonical `Enables conversion of city production to [Gold]`; Writing → the canonical `Enables establishment of embassies`; Dreamtime Pathways → a granted **Songline** promotion (`Double movement in [Desert]`).
- New objects introduced so far: 32 buildings (incl. 9 unique replacements and 3 national wonders), 4 improvements, 3 units, 1 promotion, 3 resources (Obsidian, Bitumen, Phlogiston), 1 terrain feature (Volcanic). Camp moved to O03 (Animal Behaviour Study); Stock Exchange moved to TR09 (Modern-era trade) in §7.
- Remaining foci still carry draft prose; §7.0 records the pass's status. Reward values are preliminary pending playtesting.

### v3.20 — Elemental Worlds Derived from Physics (Density Parity, Rewiring, Phase Ascendancy Dropped)

**The elemental fork is now built from the two physics models (*Elemental Physics, Models I & II*) rather than patched onto the atomic tree.**
- **Sixty-three blocked breakthroughs rewired.** The Programmable Computer and Integrated Circuit accept Resonant-State Computing beside the Transistor; Substrate Mastery (formerly Semiconductor Mastery) accepts a resonant-crystal route; Solar, Dark Matter, the Gravitational Coupling Experiment, Ion Drive, Metamaterials, Radiation Shielding, the Submarine, Interstellar Communication, Planetary Defence, Orion Pulse and Post-Scarcity all read their elemental face. Computing, Genetics, Robotics, Space Engineering and the sub-light victories are now reachable in elemental worlds; the atomic-dependency sweep is clean except for the ontologically atomic set.
- **Alternate elemental names** on shared late craft where the practical upshot is identical (Resonance / Cycle-Logic Computer, Integrated Resonator / Cycle Array, Resonant / Fire-Capture Collector, Fire / Flow Valve, Emanation Shielding, Quintessic / Overcycle Submarine).
- **Sixteen new breakthroughs, each a consequence of the models.** Shared elemental (10): Limiting Fineness (QM-E14, mirror of Wave-Particle Duality; now the gateway to Resonant-State Computing), Aetheric Electrodynamics (E15, mirror of QED), Boundary Survey (E16 — the warm ores investigated; reveals Heavy Ore, mirror of Radioactivity + Uranium), Complete Catalogue (E17, mirror of the Standard Model; second tachyonic signal), Aether Telegraphy (E18), Celestial Current Survey (E19 — now a Method F prerequisite), Quintessic Warhead (E20, mirror of Nuclear Weapons with MAD parity), Compact Athanor (E21, RARE), Medium Reservoir (E22), Current Sail (E23, UNCOMMON). Substance only (2): Formless Reserve (CH27), Dissonance Weaponry (QM-E26). Process only (4): Closed-Loop Calorimetry (CH26 — the surplus experiment, reveals the Cycle Engine), Self-Renewing Structures (QM-E24 — the Process mirror of Incorruptible Matter), Counter-Cycle Reversal (QM-E25, RARE), Phase Exchange (TR16).
- **Density parity:** a Substance world now holds ~27 elemental-exclusive Modern-and-later breakthroughs and a Process world ~29, against ~34 atomic-exclusive (excluding the deep-fission alternative path). ~512 → ~528 total.
- **Phase Ascendancy dropped.** Its cosmic-seasons form would be either a tell or dormant-until-discovered, and the universal weather mechanic that could hide it is out of scope for an Unciv fork; the Process world's Great Cycle has no terrestrial rhythm. Method F strengthened to three specific prerequisites (matching Method D's weight) with the Celestial Current Survey as its decisive signal.

### v3.19 — Cleanup Pass (Stale Text, Labels, Duplicate, Lockout Clarification)

**Pure consistency fixes arising from the v3.18 design review; no design changes.**
- **Lockout window defined as obsolescence.** The three references to a "lockout window" now say what they meant: a Revealed UNCOMMON breakthrough being obsoleted before discovery. Made explicit that only UNCOMMON breakthroughs can lock out — CORE and STANDARD never do — which reconciles the Paths Not Taken and Grand Endeavour text with §1.2's eventual-availability promise.
- **FTL sampling order corrected.** Since v3.17 the FTL candidate set depends on the Matter axis (D atomic-only, F elemental-only), so FQ9 is now sampled before FQ1; the FQ1 row, Principle 3 and §6 updated accordingly, and §6's claim that FTL selection is independent of the other axes removed. FQ6 (Biological Depth) recorded as moot in Elemental worlds.
- **Stale claims fixed:** Guarantee Rule 3 now names the Quintessic Power Plant as the Elemental-world energy fallback; the never-tell list no longer says "exactly five" methods; the Periodic Table's Elemental-world weight corrected from ~30% to ~45%.
- **Ten prerequisite labels corrected** to name the breakthrough actually cited (Fortification Design for WA06 in F15/WA24 — the intended node, not Phalanx Tactics; Archery in WA23; Atmospheric Science in RC05; Set Theory in QP13; Tribal Law in CO22 and the Culture header; Laws of Thermodynamics in the Energy header; clarifying glosses on CO09, AE01).
- **Duplicate removed:** ES01 (Water Wheel) duplicated WM05 (Water Mill) — same prerequisites, same effect. WM05 is now dual-path (also discoverable through Energy Systems). ~513 → ~512 breakthroughs; tier-philosophy bullets aligned with the statistics table.
- *Not in this pass:* the twelve Elemental-world prerequisite unblocks identified by the review (Programmable Computer, Integrated Circuit, etc.) are design changes and are deferred to the pass that derives Elemental technology from the physics model.

### v3.18 — Coherent Light in Elemental Worlds (Laser Resolution)

**Resolved the parked laser dependency: elemental worlds now field lasers by their own account, rather than being locked out of all coherent-light tech.**
- **OP07 (Laser) made theory-agnostic.** Coherent light is a *phenomenon*, dual-framed like Electricity and Relativity. Atomic worlds reach it by stimulated emission (quantum, QP02); elemental worlds by a resonant cavity forcing the aether / fire-phase into a single pure mode — a "Resonant Ray" (Substance) / "Harmonic Ray" (Process) — drawing on QM-E03 (Resonant-State Computing), the resonance mastery those worlds already use to compute. Prereq is now `OP02 + (QP02, or QM-E03)`, so lasers arrive at a comparable era in both kinds of world.
- **Downstream unblocked.** Laser-dependent tech (laser weapons, beamed power, fibre optics, and the orthodox RC03 gravitational-wave route) is now reachable in elemental worlds; the §4 note is updated accordingly. The Grand Athanor stays as an alternative tachyonic-signal carrier.
- Rationale: blocking all laser tech from non-atomic worlds hinged on the fact that stimulated emission is specifically quantum — unintuitive without graduate physics, and out of step with the Symmetry Principle that every phenomenon gets a per-ontology account. No breakthrough count change (OP07 reframed, not added).

### v3.17 — FTL in Elemental Worlds (Ontology-First Pass)

**Made faster-than-light genuinely reachable and guidable in non-atomic worlds, by reframing what the methods *are* rather than patching node lists.**
- **Capability-primitive framing.** Each FTL method is a claim about reality (space is malleable / foldable / has higher realms / matter has a superluminal mode / reality is discrete / space is a plenum). Five claims are ontology-neutral; the atomic and elemental worlds reach them through the same five **capability primitives** by different accounts, so the *research lane* of every universal method (A/B/C/E) is identical across worlds. New §4 subsection *FTL in Elemental Worlds* lays out the primitive-by-primitive mapping.
- **D/F as the matter-axis mirror.** Formalised **Method D (Quantum Tunneling) as atomic-only** (needs discreteness) and added **Method F (Plenum Drive) as elemental-only** (needs a continuous rideable medium — Aetheric/Celestial Current Drive). Each world has five candidates (four universal + its axis method), exactly one viable; six methods across all worlds.
- **Two new primitive nodes (~511→513):** QM-E12 Supernal Spheres (higher realms → Hyperspace) and QM-E13 Aetheric Vacuum (medium energy → Wormhole), both STANDARD, dual-framed.
- **Relativity dual-framed** via Lorentzian aether-mechanics — same predictions, aether-density account instead of curvature — resolving the aether-vs-relativity tension without forking the RC focus.
- **Guidance wired** across QM-E04/E06/E08/E12/E13 in elemental idiom; **fixed** the EXOTIC_MATTER token's false "all five methods reachable" claim; **extended** HIGH_ENERGY_PHYSICS to cover QP08; **updated** Guarantee Rule 2.
- Parked: whether elemental worlds field lasers at all (OP07 is defined as quantum) — broader than FTL; logged separately.

### v3.16 — Quintessic Physics: Fleshing-Out & Elemental Divergence

**Gave the elemental-world physics pillar real body and made its two traditions mechanically distinct (grounded against Unciv's actual capabilities).**
- **Four new nodes (7→11 in the focus; ~511 total).** Shared: **QM-E08 Grand Athanor / Great Observatory** (elemental Particle-Accelerator analogue — prestige + Insight; intended FTL-guidance carrier, wiring deferred). Substance-only: **QM-E09 Incorruptible Matter** (perfected, low-maintenance, durable materials — the substance materials route) and **QM-E10 Elixir of Life** (prosperity capstone: happiness, stronger Golden Ages, Great People — explicitly *not* disease/vitalism). Process-only: **QM-E11 Great Harmony (Five-Phase Balance)** (escalating bonus for holding all five phase-classes; amplifies the generative cycle).
- **Divergent identities:** substance = depth & self-sufficiency (synthesise your way out of scarcity, prosper tall); process = breadth & balance (thrive on wide, diverse, well-connected territory). Shared 7-node spine retained for the tokens and endgame.
- **Fixed a pre-existing bug in CH23**, which described "industrial districts placed in phase-adjacency" — Civ V/Unciv has no districts and buildings aren't tile-placed. Reworked to a non-spatial net-gain conversion (Cycle Forge), sidestepping both the hex-geometry and fixed-city-layout problems.
- **Dropped** the proposed sympathetic/resonant *detection* node: Civ V/Unciv's only invisible units are Submarines, detected by Destroyers/Missile Cruisers — there is no physics-sensor niche to fill.
- **Confirmed and logged** the FTL-guidance gap for elemental worlds (all guidance reports hang off atomic QP/RC nodes). Deferred as its own pass, together with a new elemental-only Method F ("Plenum Drive": Aetheric/Celestial Current Drive) mirroring the atomic-only Quantum-Tunneling method. "Phase Ascendancy" (rotating-phase tempo) parked to Phase 2.

### v3.15 — Insight Scale & Calibration

**Added an Insight Scale & Calibration note to Section 1.2 (prose-only; no breakthrough rows touched).**
- **Insight = base-game science, 1:1.** Per-turn science output is routed to Focus Areas rather than spent on a chosen tech, inheriting Unciv's balanced science scaling and using the vanilla tech-cost curve (20 → … → 9500) as the anchor for how thresholds grow across eras.
- **Per-focus Insight stored as float**, not Unciv's integer `techsInProgress` — otherwise the 20% spread's fractional per-focus share rounds to zero and never accumulates.
- **Per-city cost modifier retained, applied to the threshold.** `1 + (cities − 1) × techCostPerCityModifier` (from `TechManager.costOfTech`) is applied to breakthrough thresholds so wide empires don't out-research tall ones — preserving vanilla balance. Vanilla's `getScienceModifier` catch-up is dropped (Knowledge Diffusion replaces it).
- Noted an optional split variant (higher threshold + roll bonus = "more eyes on the problem": wide = slower-but-steadier) as a tuning idea, not adopted.

### v3.14 — Early-Game Pacing & Insight Routing

**Reworked how Insight is distributed and how the early game paces (Section 1.2), prose-only — no breakthrough rows touched.**
- **80/20 active/spread routing replaces the old building/terrain passive-Insight scheme.** The active focus takes the bulk of Insight; the remainder is spread across every other qualifying Focus Area, **weighted by environmental modifiers** (coastal → Seafaring/Trade, highland → Mining/Observation) rather than flat. Cleaner as a routing rule than the building-fed model, and the leak exists from turn 1 (before any infrastructure), which is exactly when the anti-switching-tedium property is needed. The spread thins automatically as the tree fans out, giving broad-early/concentrated-late with no decay rule.
- **Soft Specialisation-of-Labour gate.** Before SO05 the split is a gentle 50/50; discovering SO05 moves it to 80/20 (roughly doubling concentration power). Chosen over a "hard" 0/100→focus-unlock variant to avoid a dead-agency opening. Verified the SO05 pop-5 gate lands ~turn 25–40 on standard speed against Unciv's actual growth formula (`15 + 8·(pop−1) + ⌊(pop−1)^1.5⌋`, read from `CityPopulationManager.kt`), so it arrives after the Primitive CORE flurry as the tree begins to branch.
- **Front-loaded CORE** (low thresholds / high base roll in Primitive–Ancient, bursts via per-breakthrough rolls) and a **focus-queue auto-advance** UX affordance. Roll percentages left to a tuning pass.
- Necessity-modifier and serendipity wording realigned to the active/spread vocabulary.

### v3.13 — Unit Assignment + Religion-Gate Fix

- **Fixed the "Founded a Religion" prerequisite.** Civ V/Unciv seeds fewer religions than players, so a civ that never founds one would be permanently locked out. The three affected breakthroughs (CO21, CH15, CU10 — all UNIQUE/RARE, so no CORE path was broken, but the gate was still fragile and re-coupled the tree to the religion system) now require **CU02 (Monumental Architecture)** instead, keeping the ceremonial theme via an always-achievable breakthrough.
- **Mapped every base-game unit** to a breakthrough (Section 7, new Units table): 64 generic tech-gated units assigned; the 54 civ-unique units inherit the breakthrough of the generic they replace; Great People, faith-purchased Missionary/Inquisitor, and Barbarian units documented as ungated. Vanilla spaceship-part units are flagged as superseded by the FTL/sub-light victory system.


### v3.12 — Policy/Belief Cleanup + Base-Game Object Assignment

Making the tree fit a Unciv fork that **replaces the science tree** but leaves the Policy and Religion systems untouched.

- **Removed 13 breakthroughs that are really Civ V Policies, Ideologies, or Religion content** (those systems already provide them): SO07 Bureaucracy, SO12 Common Law, PH02 Ethics Systems, PH05 Political Philosophy, PH06 Humanism, PH13 Ascetic Tradition, EC01 Mercantilism, EC02 Capitalism, EC03 Central Banking, EC04 Keynesian Economics, EC05 Planned Economy, CU01 Organised Religion, CU09 Ancestor Veneration. All inbound prerequisites were repointed to surviving breakthroughs; CU01's dependants (ME16, CO21, CH15, CU10) were repointed to CU02 (Monumental Architecture), keeping the ceremonial theme without gating on the capped, competitive religion-founding mechanic.
- **Dissolved the Economics focus** (its five entries were economic-doctrine Policies). Survivors relocated to **Trade**: Bazaar Economy → TR14, Post-Scarcity Economics → TR15. Banking (SO11) no longer "unlocks Economics." Focus Areas 37 → 36.
- **Belief-redundancy audit** against Unciv's 56 Gods & Kings beliefs confirmed PH13 (Asceticism/Monasteries beliefs) and CU09 (Ancestor Worship pantheon) were duplicates; both cut. Retained techs that merely *sound* institutional but are genuine Civ V techs (Guilds, Banking, Civil Service).
- **Renames to avoid belief/worship-building name clashes:** SO02 Oral Tradition → **Storytelling** (an "Oral Tradition" pantheon exists); CO06's Cathedral building → **Basilica**; CO20's Pagoda Tower → **Seismic Pagoda**.
- **New Section 7 — Base-Game Object Assignments:** every Gods & Kings wonder (40 world + 14 national), building (70), and tech-gated tile improvement (20) is mapped to the breakthrough that unlocks it, with the original `requiredTech` recorded for provenance. Great-Person improvements and faith-purchased worship buildings are documented as ungated.


### v3.11 — Civ V / Unciv Platform Fit: Health Removed, Marsh Signal Removed

A pass to ensure every effect is expressible in the target platform (a fork of Unciv / Civ V, with the science tech tree replaced by the Focus/Insight system).

- **The "health" stat is gone.** Civ V and Unciv have no health stat (that was Civ IV); the platform uses **happiness** (empire-wide) and **food/growth** (per city). All ~30 uses of "+health / +city health / +population health" across the tree were converted: medical and sanitation effects now read as **+happiness** (or **+growth / +food** where the effect was about city size), and fossil-fuel pollution now reads as **−happiness** rather than −health. Only the ordinary English word "health" remains (e.g. "holistic health system"), never as a stat.
- **The marsh signal is removed.** The v3.10 approach — a baseline health penalty on marsh/fen tiles in miasma worlds from turn one — was both unimplementable (no health stat) and a *diagnostic tell*: an experienced player could read "less-healthy-than-expected marshes" as "this is a miasma world" long before the decisive experiment. FQ10 now gives **every** disease world the same underlying pressure (plague events, growth penalties in large cities); only the *counter* differs, and nothing about the counter is visible until MD15 resolves the axis. MD16 (Miasma Engineering) now simply grants the miasma world's counter — a Sanitation Works building (+happiness, removes the growth-penalty) and marsh-drainage that yields a happiness bonus other worlds don't get — with no retroactive terrain effect.
- **Design rule updated** accordingly: a discovery must never become a diagnostic tell for an unresolved axis. The illustrative example switched from the (now-removed) deadly-marsh case to resource reveals and the disease axis.
- **Platform-fit note on Insight and on governance/religion entries** — see discussion; Insight is the mod's custom resource replacing "science spent toward a chosen tech" and is retained. Several governance/economic and faith entries are mechanically legal as Unciv Techs (a Tech can carry empire-wide Uniques) but are more idiomatic as Policies/Beliefs; that reclassification is tracked separately as a design decision, not a correctness fix.

### v3.10 — Discovery Reveals Reality, Never Rewrites It

Fixed a category error in how a Truth's consequences were modelled, and codified the rule so it can't recur.

- **MD16 (Miasma Engineering) reworked.** It previously *created* terrain health effects on discovery ("marshes dangerous, highlands healthy") — as if learning the theory made marshes deadly and highlands salubrious. But the miasma was always there; the discovery is knowledge, not a change to the world. MD16 now *reveals and counters* a pre-existing danger: it removes the marsh-miasma health penalty through drainage/purification and lets you weaponise foul air. It no longer touches highlands at all (nothing about them changed).
- **Baseline marsh danger moved to the FQ10 world condition.** In a Miasma world, stagnant low ground carries an unexplained health penalty **from game start**, independent of whether miasma theory has been discovered. The threat predates the knowledge.
- **New durable design rule** added to Section 5 ("Discovery reveals reality; it never rewrites it"): breakthrough effects must be framed as knowledge, capability, or infrastructure — never as terrain/resource/map changes that switch on at discovery. Prefer *reveals/enables/counters/exploits* over *makes/becomes/turns X dangerous*.
- Audited the other Truth axes and all decisive-experiment breakthroughs; MD16 was the only offender. The rest already used reveal/enable/validate framing.

### v3.9 — Rehomed IDs Renumbered & Signal Fires → Fire

Follow-up to the v3.8 dissolution: the nine rehomed breakthroughs kept their old `CM` IDs in v3.8; they are now renumbered to match their host foci, and one moved home.

- **Signal Fire Network moved from Warfare to Fire** — signal fires are, self-evidently, fire. (It keeps its military effects and WA02 prerequisite; it's simply flavoured and filed under Fire now.)
- **All nine renumbered to their host-focus prefixes** (every reference updated in lockstep): Postal System `CM01`→**TR13**; Semaphore `CM02`→**OP10**; Telephone `CM04`→**EL13**; Radio `CM05`→**EN11**; Satellite Communications `CM06`→**RK16**; Quantum Communication `CM07`→**QP14**; Interstellar Communication `CM08`→**SE14**; Talking Drum Networks `CM09`→**CU13**; Signal Fire Network `CM10`→**F15**.
- The internal chain (Radio → Satellite → Quantum → Interstellar Communication) and the Mass Media (CU08) dependency were updated to the new IDs and re-verified. No `CM` IDs remain in the live tree; the `CM` prefix is retired.

### v3.8 — Communications Focus Dissolved

The Communications focus was too sparse to be a legible investment — most of its breakthroughs were already dual-listed with other foci, and the era gaps made it hard to know when entering it was worthwhile. Rather than pad it out, it has been dissolved and its breakthroughs rehomed.

- **Removed the five v3.6 filler techs** (CM11 Courier Relay, CM12 Cartographic Standardisation, CM13 Printing & Broadsheets, CM14 Encrypted Signals, CM15 Digital Networks).
- **Relocated the nine original breakthroughs** to their most natural foci (prerequisite chains and the Mass Media dependency kept intact): Postal System → **Trade**; Semaphore → **Optics**; Telephone → **Electricity**; Radio → **Electronics**; Satellite Communications → **Rocketry**; Quantum Communication → **Quantum Physics**; Interstellar Communication → **Space Engineering**; Talking Drum Networks (UNIQUE) → **Religion & Culture**; Signal Fire Network (UNIQUE) → **Warfare** (later moved to Fire in v3.9).
- **Deleted the Communications focus.** Focus Areas 38 → 37. Dead dual-path notes on MC06 (Printing Press) and EL04 (Telegraph) removed, as those were the "also via Communications" pointers.
- Net breakthrough count 525 → 520.

### v3.7 — Disease Forks, Cross-Listing Index & Changelog Relocation

- **Antibiotics (MD06) made Germ-exclusive.** Antibiotics target microbes, so they now require Germ Theory to have validated (FQ10 = Germ). Miasma and Humoral worlds reach empire-wide disease control through their own routes instead.
- **Humoral tech line filled out.** Previously only MD17 existed for humoral worlds. Added MD18 (Humoral Pharmacology — the humoral mass-medicine equivalent of antibiotics), MD19 (Temperament Engineering — cultivate a city's dominant humour for civic bonuses), and MD-V01 (Vital Essence Medicine — a vitalist medicine layer, dual-listed with Genetics).
- **MASS_MEDICINE token** added so downstream medicine (e.g. Gene Therapy MD11) no longer hard-depends on the germ-only Antibiotics; it is satisfied by MD06 (germ), MD16 (miasma), or MD18 (humoral).
- **Vitalist AI route dual-listed.** GN14 (Animate Intelligence) is now reachable through Genetics *or* Computing, matching the existing bio-computing dual-paths.
- **Per-focus cross-listing index.** Every focus that can discover a breakthrough whose home is another focus now carries an explicit note under its availability line listing those dual-path breakthroughs and their home focus — so a player browsing one focus can see everything reachable through it.
- **Changelog moved to the end of the document** (was at the top), so the design content opens immediately after the title.

### v3.6 — Non-Atomic Coherence Pass

Follow-up sweep making the alternative-physics forks hang together across the whole tree, plus fixes.

- **Aeronautics reworked for Phlogiston worlds.** Levity flight (F13) exists from the Early Modern era, so a phlogiston civilisation enters Aeronautics already airborne: AE01 (Hot Air Balloon) is skipped, AE02 (Airship) arrives early and cheaply, and F13 is now a focus-entry route. "Zeppelin Resurgence" (which made no sense where airships never lapsed) is now **AE12 Grand Aerostat**, dual-framed — a *resurgence* in atomic worlds, the *culmination of an unbroken tradition* in phlogiston worlds. Identical mechanics.
- **Rocketry given elemental equivalents.** RK08/RK10/RK13/RK14 now resolve via the HIGH_ENERGY_PHYSICS token and carry alternate names/flavour for elemental worlds (Quintessic Thermal Rocket, Great Work Drive, Alchemical Pulse Drive). No mechanical change.
- **Quantum Physics table formatting fixed** (missing blank line before the table).
- **Communications thickened** from one tech per era to a steady chain: added CM11 Courier Relay Network, CM12 Cartographic Standardisation, CM13 Printing & Broadsheets, CM14 Encrypted Signals, CM15 Digital Networks. It is now legible as a focus worth entering.
- **Quintessic Physics de-Hellenised.** All seven QM-E breakthroughs now carry dual framings — a **Substance** reading (Greek/Aristotelian aether) and a **Process** reading (Chinese *wu xing* cycles) — with shared mechanics, so the elemental pillar serves both FQ9 alternative values rather than just alchemy.
- **Vitalism made compatible with petrochemistry & bio-organic synthesis.** Only *abiotic* synthesis is blocked in vitalist worlds; chemistry on once-living feedstock (petroleum, biomass) carries residual vital essence and works normally. CH25, CH07, and CH09-W updated.
- **Vitalism now blocks silicon AGI.** In vitalist worlds intelligence is a property of life: the silicon route (EN06) dead-ends as narrow AI, and general intelligence requires a living substrate — GN11 (grown neural architecture) or the new **GN14 Animate Intelligence** (vital-essence homunculus mind). CP09 is now Truth-aware.
- **Energy Systems gained non-atomic plants:** ES15 Quintessic Power Plant (elemental), ES16 Caloric Engine Plant, ES17 Phlogiston Furnace Plant — filling the grid-power niche that previously only fission/fusion covered.
- **Materials Science & Electricity checked for non-atomic compatibility.** MS02 renamed **Nanotechnology / Fine Structuring** and routed through the HIGH_ENERGY_PHYSICS token; a convergence note explains most of the focus is shared post-reconciliation. Electricity gained a theory-agnostic note (it is phenomenon-level; the *account* of current differs by world, the tech does not).

### v3.5 — The Symmetry Principle: No Privileged Reality

**Our reality is no longer the default.** Previously the orthodox Truth values (atomic matter, kinetic heat, mechanist life, germ disease) were treated as the baseline tree, with alternatives "validating or dead-ending" off it. This was philosophically inconsistent: those orthodox answers are as contingent as any rival, and they gate their own exclusive content that alternative worlds lack. The **Symmetry Principle** makes every Truth value — including ours — a fork that opens an exclusive cluster and forecloses its rivals'.

**Foundational axes now "fork" rather than "dead-end."** A losing theory routes the civilisation onto a genuinely different chain occupying the same **structural niche** (energy pillar, materials pillar, computation substrate, medicine paradigm), never a consolation prize. Dead ends are reserved for *frontier* axes, where "impossible in this universe" is a legitimate answer. Added a Niche Equivalents table mapping each pillar's orthodox and alternative forks.

**New pillar — Quintessic Physics (QM-E01–07).** The elemental-world structural mirror of Nuclear Physics: in Elemental worlds (FQ9 ≠ Corpuscular) the atomic→quantum→semiconductor spine never coheres, so Quintessic Physics fills the niche — Quintessence Theory, Elemental Binding Energy (Quintessic Reactor), Resonance/Cycle-Logic Computing, Aetheric Field Theory, Great Work Reactor, Aetheric Exotic Matter, Philosopher's Engine. Elemental worlds reach FTL through this chain.

**Deep-physics tokens.** HIGH_ENERGY_PHYSICS (NP02 or QM-E01) and EXOTIC_MATTER (QP11, QM-E06, or the phlogiston route) let the late tree accept either foundation without duplicating downstream rows. Nuclear/Quantum and Quintessic Physics are mutually exclusive per world, selected by FQ9.

**Far-flung cross-axis implications.** Formalised: Phlogiston → Exotic Matter (new F14 Levity Condensate — a Classical chemistry Truth reaching the FTL endgame a full era early), Elemental → Deterministic quantum foundation & non-silicon computation, Caloric → vacuum-energy/fusion-containment bias, Vitalist → quantum-coherent biology bias. All disciplined by master-axis derivation so no contradictory world generates.

**Vitalist & caloric forks formalised** as the materials and energy-storage niche equivalents (CH07 marked as the FQ11 fork point; GN13 bridges to Materials Science; TH11/TH12 fill the caloric energy niche).

**Redundancy/inconsistency fixes:** removed duplicate Ekranoplan (AE10 repurposed as Flying Boat; SF15 made dual-path); de-duplicated Factory (CO10 now a Factory *upgrade* over MC08's base), Zero-Point Generator (ES12 now a grid-scale *Reactor* upgrade of QP12's prototype), and Laboratory (PH04 now grants the Research Institute upgrade over CH01's Laboratory).

### v3.4 — Structural Merge & Coherence Pass

**World Truths unified.** The former Section 5 ("Alternative Technology Paths") and Section 5B ("World Truths") were two descriptions of one system, using two vocabularies. They are now a single Section 5 ("World Truths — Variable Laws of Physics"). The six original narrative worlds (No-Gunpowder, No-Fusion, No-Fossil-Fuels, Early Steam, Enhanced Springs, Antikythera) are folded in as **Worked Examples** of the Geological (Removal) and Historical (Enabling) families, nested under the general taxonomy. "World Flag" is retired as a system name (retained only as individual Truth identifiers like `GUNPOWDER_ABSENT`); the unifying term is **World Truth** throughout. Section 6's balance-rules summary and all cross-references retargeted accordingly. No breakthrough rows, effects, or strategic-experience content were altered in the merge — only section structure and terminology.

### v3.3 — Discovery States, Grand Endeavours & World Truths

**Three-state discovery model (Section 1.4):** Every breakthrough is Unknown → Revealed → Discovered per civilisation. Unknown = invisible, serendipitous rolls only. Revealed = known-possible, visible as a named goal. Revelation triggers: theoretical (theory reveals its direct applications — rare before Scientific Method, the norm after), proximity (formalised "promising developments"), observational (seeing another civ's breakthrough in effect), espionage. **One-Step Horizon rule:** revelation never reaches beyond direct applications of the frontier — nobody in 1950 glimpses the smartphone. Added serendipitous cross-focus accidents (penicillin moments) and the Paths Not Taken end-of-game codex.

**Grand Endeavours (Section 1.5):** Revealed breakthroughs with Discovered prerequisites can be pursued as directed programmes with visible progress and guaranteed completion, at a 2× (v3.112; 2.5–3× before) Insight premium (4× RARE). One at a time; occupies the focus slot; suspends the target's obsolescence (UNCOMMON only); spin-off discoveries at milestones. Models the fusion-programme mode of real research. The final FTL launch is always a Grand Endeavour — evidence identifies the target, engineering achieves it.

**World Truths system (Section 5):** Variable laws of physics, sampled per game. Three layers: **phenomena and craft** are fixed (fire burns, metals smelt — everywhere); **mechanisms** are sampled from the Classical era on (Foundational Questions); **frontiers** are sampled in the Atomic/Space eras (fusion, superconductivity, vacuum energy, FTL). Twelve Truth Axes total (5 Foundational + 7 Frontier), each resolved by a decisive experiment that exists in every game. Six anti-explosion principles: disjoint clusters, shared content with validate/dead-end outcomes, master-axis derivation, bounded axis count (~30,000 physics signatures from ~75 authored outcomes), constraint-checked sampling, and **Deep Reconciliation** (foundational alternatives reconnect to shared late-game physics while keeping their exclusive unlocks). Understanding Tokens (COMBUSTION/MATTER/DISEASE/LIFE/THERMO_THEORY) let downstream prereqs reference understanding rather than a specific theory, so the practical tree runs identically whatever the deep laws.

**The five Foundational Questions:** FQ8 Combustion (oxygen / phlogiston-real → early airships), FQ9 Matter (corpuscular / substance-elemental Greek transmutation / process-elemental Chinese wu xing cycles), FQ10 Disease (germ / miasma / humoral), FQ11 Nature of Life (mechanist / vitalist → vital essence, animate constructs), FQ12 Nature of Heat (kinetic / caloric → heat as a stored, traded, weaponised substance). Vitalism corrected out of the disease axis (it concerns the living/dead boundary, not pathology) and given its own life-sciences axis; humoral theory added as the historically dominant disease rival.

**New chains:** Phlogiston (F12–F13), Greek transmutation (CH20–CH21), Chinese five-phase (CH22 Five-Phase Theory, CH23 Generative Cycle Engine, CH24 Phase-Destruction Warfare), miasma (MD16), humoral (MD17), vitalist (GN12 Vital Essence Isolation, GN13 Animating Principle), caloric (TH09 Caloric Theory, TH11 Caloric Reservoir, TH12 Caloric Projection), gravitic (RC08–RC10), and decisive experiments (CH18, CH19, CH25, MD15, TH10). Candidate future axes noted (light, aether, cosmology, spontaneous generation).

**Guarantee rules 11–12 added:** constraint-checked world sampling; victory never dice-locked (FTL prereqs always Revealable and Endeavour-able).

### v3.2 — Discovery System Rewrite, UNIQUE Overhaul & Balance Fixes

**Discovery system rewritten:**
- Tiers now affect discovery *difficulty* (roll chance), not existence. Every breakthrough is present in every game unless a World Flag removes it.
- CORE: easy roll, excess Insight can reach 100%. STANDARD: fairly easy, high ceiling but never 100%. UNCOMMON: moderate, with obsolescence mechanic. RARE: hard, low ceiling — exciting when found, never required.
- RARE gateway chains: follow-up breakthroughs promoted to STANDARD. Applies to Clockwork (MC16/17/18), Antikythera (O13/14/15), and Bio-Computing (GN09/10/11) chains.
- World Flags split into Removal Flags (remove breakthroughs) and Enabling Flags (lower RARE gateway difficulty).

**UNIQUE tier overhauled (56 breakthroughs, up from 15):**
- Same base roll difficulty as STANDARD, but cumulative penalty per UNIQUE already discovered (natural limit of 2–5 per civ).
- First-come-first-served: once any civ discovers a UNIQUE, all others permanently locked out. Cannot be traded or stolen.
- Every UNIQUE has prerequisites (usually environmental) and pushes the player toward a specific playstyle by amplifying the value of its prereqs.
- 41 new UNIQUEs added across 20 focus areas, covering every environmental niche.

**Duplicate/overlap cleanup:**
- Removed FA04 (Terrace Farming) and FA05 (Rice Paddy Cultivation) — absorbed by UNIQUE counterparts FA15 and WM09. Basic hill/wet-terrain farming folded into FA01.
- Fixed building name overlaps (TX12, AH05, TR11, EL09/EN11). Added missing prereq chains (F15→F05, TR11→TR03, TX12→TX02).

**Obsolescence mechanic (UNCOMMON breakthroughs):**
- 10 obsolescence relationships. -W alternatives always exist but usually obsoleted. World Flags protect them.

**Infrastructure additions:**
- Added CO23 (Railway Network, CORE): standalone breakthrough for railways, previously scattered as building mentions across MC08/ME12/CO10. Requires Steam Engine + Road Building + Blast Furnace.
- Added WM11 (Industrial Canal Network, UNCOMMON): bulk inland transport enabling early industrialisation. Obsoleted by CO23 (Railway Network) — canals give a powerful window before railways arrive. River biome required.
- Cleaned up MC08, ME12, CO10 to reference CO23 properly instead of granting railway buildings directly.

**Vague prerequisite cleanup:**
- Replaced all vague prereqs ("specific conditions", "extended research", "basic crafting", "direct observation", "sufficient population", "appropriate terrain") with concrete breakthrough IDs and environmental conditions across 16 breakthroughs and 6 focus area unlock conditions.

**Knowledge Diffusion, Strategic Resource Access, bug fixes** — see previous changelog entries.

- **Restored dual-path discovery**: ~15 breakthroughs can now be discovered through multiple Focus Areas (e.g. Gunpowder through Chemistry or Warfare, Printing Press through Mechanics or Communications, Vaccination through Medicine or Animal Husbandry)
- **New speculative path: No Fossil Fuels World** (FOSSIL_FUELS_ABSENT flag) — Coal, petroleum, and bitumen never appear. Added ME08-W (Charcoal Blast Furnace), MC09-W (Alcohol Combustion Engine), CH09-W (Bio-Organic Synthesis), ES03-W (Biomass Power Plant), ES05-W (Alcohol/Biogas Power Plant), MI13 (Wood Gas Production). All downstream prereqs updated to accept substitutes.
- **New speculative path: Enhanced Springs / Advanced Clockwork** (ENHANCED_SPRINGS flag) — Added MC15 (Mainspring Energy Storage), MC16 (Clockwork Automaton), MC17 (Spring-Powered Vehicle), MC18 (Clockwork Analytical Engine). Creates a "clockpunk" industrialization path.
- **New speculative path: Antikythera Chain** (ANTIKYTHERA_TRADITION flag) — Added O13 (Mechanical Ephemeris), O14 (Tide Computing Engine), O15 (Mechanical Weather Predictor). Each reduces cost of downstream mechanical computing breakthroughs.
- **Formalized World Flags system** with probabilities and combination effects
- **Updated MC14 (Babbage)** to receive cost reductions from both Antikythera and Clockwork chains
- **Updated Guaranteed Path Integrity rules** (10 rules, up from 7) ensuring all world flag combinations maintain viable paths to victory

### v3.0 — Core Game Design Pass
- **Removed MI01 (Copper Extraction), MI02 (Tin Extraction), MI03 (Gold Panning), MI04 (Iron Ore Extraction)** as separate breakthroughs. These were redundant busywork.
- **S04 (Ore Identification)** now reveals all surface mineral deposits on the map — a visible, exciting moment. Copper, tin, gold, and iron deposits all appear.
- **F04 (Smelting)** now enables extraction and smelting of soft metals (copper, tin, gold) from revealed deposits. No separate extraction step needed.
- **Iron** remains gated behind ME-B (Bellows) + F03 (Charcoal) — the temperature barrier is the meaningful challenge, not finding the ore.
- **MI05 (Deep Shaft Mining)** reveals additional *deep* deposits not visible on the surface — a second exciting map-reveal moment.
- Updated all downstream references to removed breakthroughs.

### Duplicate Removal
- **Removed CH03 (Gunpowder Composition)** — duplicate of WA10. Gunpowder is now discoverable through *either* Chemistry or Warfare focus, noted on WA10.
- **Removed CM03 (Telegraph)** — duplicate of EL04. Telegraph is now discoverable through *either* Communications or Electricity focus, noted on EL04.
- **Removed ES02 (Windmill)** — duplicate of MC04. MC04 now noted as discoverable through Energy Systems or Mechanics.
- **Removed MS06 (Room Temp Superconductor)** — duplicate of EL12. EL12 now noted as reachable through Electronics or Materials Science.

### Gameplay Effects Added
Every breakthrough now has a tangible gameplay reason to be excited about it, beyond just fulfilling prerequisites. Major additions:
- **All "pure prereq" science breakthroughs** (MA08, MA10, MA13, QP01-QP08, RC01-RC02, NP01-NP02, PH01, PH03, PH10, etc.) now have concrete gameplay effects: buildings, unit improvements, map reveals, or strategic abilities.
- **Mining resources** (Saltpeter, Sulfur, Bitumen, Volcanic Ash, Rubber, Rare Earths) now each provide immediate gameplay benefits beyond being prerequisites.
- **Construction and infrastructure** breakthroughs given clearer strategic value.
